General Notes for Package Units:
Package Units are self-contained, all-in-one units consisting of, but not limited to: Equipment, interconnecting piping, electrical, instrument components, steel structures... already captured under the weight below established.
Package Units are fabricated and skid-mounted in one or more sections, with loose items that shall be required to be completed at SITE.
Package Units are generally installed in a unique location, although some loose items or auxiliary systems may be installed in isolated or dedicated foundations / supporting steel structures.

1.The labor rales included herein are based on the straight time hours based on sixty (60) hours per week, consisting of ten (10)
hours/day, Saturday through Monday, but are also applicable to any additional and/or nighttime hours worked.
2.The labor rates are "all-in" rales and include wages, insurance, benefits, taxes, social burdens, board and lodging, transportation costs,
recruitment costs, payroll burdens, vacation pay, holiday pay, sick pay, overheads and profits, hand tools (such as grinder, cutter, drill,
chain block, and the like),consumables, general and administrative expenses as per Wison/ADNOC's policy, mobilization and
demobilization to and from the Site/office, idle time, stand by time and shall apply regardless of the working location of category of
manpower.
3.For Subcontractor's and its low tier subcontractor's personnel, the labor rates above shall be deemed to include all wages, payroll
burdens, benefits, applicable home office and field overheads (including computers, communications and reprographics, elc.mobilizalion
and demobilization, site accommodation, meals, travel & transporlalion and related expenses, whether direct or indirect.
4.In case Wison requires Subcontractor to supply material as requested,a markup percentage of 5% (five percent) will be added to the
net cost (delivered to site) of the materials supplied by the Subcontractor.
5. VAT will be excluded in the proposal and submit together with the inovice, which is required to be approved by Wison.

1.The above daily equipment rates shall apply for 10 hours/day of operational use of Subcontractor-owned and supplier's
construction equipment which has been mobilized to the general vicinity of WORK SITE. And the monthly rate applies for the
equipment which to be rented for at least 1 month. When Subcontractor's equipment does not resemble the equipment having
rates listed herein, the rate shall be negotiated and agreed upon in writing by Contractor.
2.Equipment rates shall include all costs to Subcontractor for mobilization, demobilization, loading and off-loading, assembly and
disassembly, overtime premium, bonuses, holidays premium, fuel, maintenance, repairs, insurance, overhead and profits, and
any other allowance and/or expense incurred to Subcontractor in compliance with all applicable Federal, State and Local laws.
3.Equipment rates shall include all typical equipment accessories required for use within the equipment including spare parts.
4.Any equipment standby time for changes in SUBCONTRACT WORK caused by CONTRACTOR shall be approved by
CONTRACTOR (if applicable), and shall be paid at the percentages quoted above.
5.Subcontractor's costs for any construction equipment not available at jobsite which must be rented by Subcontractor or its low
tier subcontractor or suppliers for the execution of changes to SUBCONTRACT WORK shall be reimbursed at actual cost to
Subcontractor plus.5%-(five percent).
6.The cost for equipment drivers, operators, fuelers, oilers etc. are included in the Construction Equipment rates.
7. VAT will be excluded in the proposal and submit together with the invoice, which is required to be approved by Wison.
